int((secs % 1) * 100)\n    return f\"{hours}:{minutes:02d}:{int(secs):02d}.{cs:02d}\"\n\ndef generate_rttm(diarization, audio_duration):\n    \"\"\"Generate RTTM format output.\"\"\"\n    rttm_lines = []\n    for turn, _, speaker in diarization.itertracks(yield_label=True):\n        start = turn.start\n        duration = turn.end - turn.start\n        # Format: SPEAKER file_name channel_num start_time duration <NA> <NA> speaker_id <NA> <NA>\n        speaker_id = speaker.replace(\"SPEAKER_\", \"spk\")\n        rttm_line = f\"SPEAKER input 1 {start:.3f} {duration:.3f} <NA> <NA> {speaker_id} <NA> <NA>\"\n        rttm_lines.append(rttm_line)\n    return rttm_lines\n\ndef generate_ass_subtitles(result_segments, audio_duration):\n    \"\"\"Generate ASS subtitle format with \"\",\n        \"[Events]\",\n        \"Format: Layer, Start, End, Style, Name, MarginL, MarginR, MarginV, Effect, Text\"\n    ]\n\n    if \"segments\" in result_segments:\n        for seg in seg.get(\"speaker\", \"SPEAKER_00\")\n\n            if not text:\n                continue\n\n            start_fmt = format_ass_time(start)\n            end_fmt = format_ass_time(end)\n            text_with_speaker = f\"{speaker}:{text}\"\n            dialogue = f\"Dialogue: 0,{start_fmt},{end_fmt},Default,,0,0,0,,{text_with_speaker}\"\n            ass_lines.append(dialogue)\n\n    return ass_lines\n\ndef calculate_speech_time(diarization):\n    \"\"\"Calculate total speech time from diarization.\"\"\"\n    total_time = 0\n    for turn, _, _ in diarization.itertracks():\n        total_time += turn.end - turn.start\n    return total_time\n\ndef main():\n    steps_completed = []\n    libraries_used = [\"whisperx\", \"torch\", \"pyannote.audio\", \"numpy\"]\n    tools_used = {}\n\n    # Step 1: Extract audio\n    print(\"Step 1: Extracting audio...\")\n    try:\n        audio_duration = extract_audio(VIDEO_PATH, AUDIO_PATH)\n {audio_duration:.2f}s\")\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error extracting audio: {e}\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    # Step 2: Run transcription\n    print(\"\\nStep 2: Running tools_used[\"transcription\"] = f\"OpenAI Whisper 'base' model (device: {device}, compute_type: {compute_type})\"\n        print(\"Transcription complete\")\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error in transcription: {e}\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    # Step 3: Run diarization\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Running speaker diarization...\")\n    try:\n        diarization = embedding)\"\n        num_speakers = len(set([s for _, _, s in diarization.itertracks()]))\n        print(f\"Detected {num_speakers} speakers\")\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error in Step 4: Calculate total speech time\n    total_speech_time = 0\n    for turn, _, _ in diarization.itertracks():\n        total_speech_time += turn.end - turn.start\n\n    # Step 5: Generate RTTM\n    print(\"\\nStep 4: Generating RTTM file...\")\n    rttm_lines = generate_rttm(diarization, audio_duration, num_speakers)\n    rttm_path = f\"{OUTPUT_DIR}/diarization.rttm\"\n    with open(rttm_path, \"w\") as f:\n        f.write(\"\\n\".join(rttm_lines))\n    print(f\"RTTM saved to {rttm_path}\")\n\n    # Step 6: Merge and generate ASS\n    print(\"\\nStep 5: Generating ASS subtitles...\")\n    merged_segments = merge_diarization_transcription(diarization, transcription)\n    ass_lines = generate_ass_subtitles(merged_segments, audio_duration)\n    ass_path = f\"{OUTPUT_DIR}/subtitles.ass\"\n    with open(ass_path, \"w\") as f:\n        f.write(\"\\n\".join(ass_lines))\n    print(f\"ASS saved to {ass_path}\")\n\n    # Step 7: Generate report\n    print(\"\\nStep 6: Generating report...\")\n    report = {\n        \"num_speakers_pred\": num_speakers,\n        \"total_speech_time_sec\": round(total_speech_time, 2),\n        \"audio_duration_sec\": round(audio_duration, 2),\n        \"steps_completed\": steps_completed,\n        \"commands_used\": [\"ffmpeg\", \"python3\"],\n        \"libraries_used\": libraries_used,\n        \"tools_used\": tools_used,\n        \"notes\": \"Used pyannote.audio 3.1 for speaker diarization and OpenAI Whisper base model for transcription. Audio extracted at 16kHz mono for optimal performance.\"\n    }\n\n    report_path = f\"{OUTPUT_DIR}/report.json\"\n    with open(report_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"Report saved to {report_path}\")\n\n    print(\"\\n=== Processing Complete ===\")\n    print(f\"Detected {num_speakers} speakers\")\n    print(f\"Total speech time: {total_speech_time:.2f}s / {audio_duration:.2f}s\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"}}

audio_duration):\n    \"\"\"Generate RTTM format output.\"\"\"\n    rttm_lines = []\n    for turn, _, speaker in diarization.itertracks(yield_label=True):\n        start = turn.start\n        duration = turn.end - turn.start\n        # Format: SPEAKER file_name channel_num start_time duration <NA> <NA> speaker_id <NA> <NA>\n        speaker_id = speaker.replace(\"SPEAKER_\", \"spk\")\n  for seg in segments:\n        start = seg.get(\"start\", 0)\n        end = seg.get(\"end\", 0)\n        text = seg.get(\"text\", \"\").strip()\n\n        if not text or start is None:\n            continue\n\n        # Find the dominant speaker in this segment\n        segment = Segment(start, end)\n        speaker_labels = {}\n        for turn, _, speaker in diarization.itertracks(yield_label=True):\n            overlap = segment.duration - segment.crop(turn).duration\n            if overlap > 0:\n                label = speaker\n                speaker_labels[label] = speaker_labels.get(label, 0) + overlap\n\n        # Get the speaker with maximum overlap\n        if speaker_labels:\n            dominant_speaker = max(speaker_labels,       merged.append({\n            \"start\": start,\n            \"end\": end,\n            \"speaker\": speaker_id,\n            \"text\": text\n        })\n\n    return merged\n\ndef calculate_speech_time(diarization):\n    \"\"\"Calculate total speech time from diarization.\"\"\"\n    total_time = 0\n    for turn, _, _ in diarization.itertracks():\n        total_time += turn.end - turn.start\n    return total_time\n\ndef main():\n    steps_completed = []\n    libraries_used = [\"openai-whisper\", \"torch\", \"pyannote.audio\", \"numpy\", \"librosa\"]\n    tools_used = {}\n\n Found {num_speakers} speakers\")\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error in speaker assignment: {e}\")\n        import traceback\n        traceback.print_exc()\n        num_speakers = len(set([s for _, _, s in diarization.itertracks()]))\n        merged_segments = []\n\n    # Step 5: Calculate total speech time\n    total_speech_time = calculate_speech_time(diarization)\n\n    # Step 6: Generate RTTM\n    print(\"\\nStep 5: Generating RTTM file...\")\n    rttm_lines = generate_rttm(diarization, audio_duration)\n    rttm_path = f\"{OUTPUT_DIR}/diarization.rttm\"\n    with open(rttm_path, \"w\") as f:\n        f.write(\"\\n\".join(rttm_lines))\n    print(f\"RTTM saved to {rttm_path}\")\n\n    # Step 7: Generate ASS subtitles\n    print(\"\\nStep 6: Generating ASS subtitles...\")\n    ass_lines = generate_ass_subtitles(merged_segments, audio_duration)\n    ass_path = f\"{OUTPUT_DIR}/subtitles.ass\"\n    with open(ass_path, \"w\") as f:\n        f.write(\"\\n\".join(ass_lines))\n    print(f\"ASS saved to {ass_path}\")\n\n    # Step 8: Generate report\n    print(\"\\nStep 7: Generating report...\")\n    report = {\n        \"num_speakers_pred\": num_speakers,\n        \"total_speech_time_sec\": round(total_speech_time, 2),\n        \"audio_duration_sec\": round(audio_duration, 2),\n        \"steps_completed\": steps_completed,\n        \"commands_used\": [\"ffmpeg\", \"python3\"],\n        \"libraries_used\": libraries_used,\n        \"tools_used\": tools_used,\n        \"notes\": \"Used HuggingFace Transformers Whisper base model for transcription and pyannote.audio 3.1 for speaker diarization. Audio extracted at 16kHz mono for optimal performance.\"\n    }\n\n    report_path = f\"{OUTPUT_DIR}/report.json\"\n    with open(report_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"Report saved to {report_path}\")\n\n    print(\"\\n=== Processing Complete ===\")\n    print(f\"Detected {num_speakers} speakers\")\n    print(f\"Total speech time: {total_speech_time:.2f}s / {audio_duration:.2f}s\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"}}

python3\n\"\"\"\nSpeaker Diarization Pipeline - Simple VAD-based approach\nExtracts audio, performs transcription and simple diarization, and generates output files.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nimport os\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport librosa\n\n# Configuration\nAUDIO_PATH = \"/root/audio.wav\"\nVIDEO_PATH = \"/root/input.mp4\"\nOUTPUT_DIR = \"/root\"\n\ndef get_audio_duration(audio_path):\n    \"\"\"Get audio duration using ffmpeg.\"\"\"\n    result = subprocess.run(\n        [\"ffprobe\", \"-v\", \"error\", \"-show_entries\", \"format=duration\", \"-of\", \"default=noprint_wrappers=1:nokey=1\", audio_path],\n        capture_output=True, text=True\n    )\n    return float(result.stdout.strip())\n\ndef extract_audio(video_path, audio_path):\n    \"\"\"Extract audio from video using ffmpeg.\"\"\"\n    cmd = [\"ffmpeg\", \"-y\", \"-i\", video_path, \"-ac\", \"1\", \"-ar\", \"16000\", audio_path]\n    subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, check=True)\n    return get_audio_duration(audio_path)\n\ndef run_transcription(audio_path):\n    \"\"\"Run transcription using OpenAI Whisper.\"\"\"\n    import whisper\n\n    device = \"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\"\n    print(f\"Using device: {device}\")\n\n    # Load Whisper model\n    model = whisper.load_model(\"base\")\n    audio, sr = librosa.load(audio_path, sr=16000)\n\n    # Transcribe with timestamps\n    result = model.transcribe(audio, word_timestamps=True)\n\n    return result, device\n\ndef run_simple_diarization(audio_path, num_speakers=2):\n    \"\"\"Run simple VAD-based speaker diarization using audio energy.\"\"\"\n    import torch\n    from pyannote.core import Annotation, Segment\n\n    # Load audio\n    audio, sr = librosa.load(audio_path, sr=16000)\n\n    # Compute energy for VAD\n    frame_length = 2048\n    hop_length = 512\n    energy = np.array([np.sum(audio[i:i+frame_length]**2) for i in range(0, len(audio)-frame_length, hop_length)])\n\n    # Adaptive threshold based on audio characteristics\n    if np.any(energy > 0):\n        threshold = np.mean(energy[energy > np.percentile(energy[energy > 0], 5)])\n    else:\n        threshold = 0\n\n    speech_frames = energy > threshold\n\n    # Create diarization annotation with speaker switching\n    diarization = Annotation()\n    current_speaker = 0\n    in_speech = False\n    start_time = 0\n    min_speech_duration = 0.5  # Minimum speech segment duration in seconds\n    max_silence_duration = 1.5  # Maximum silence before switching speakers\n\n    last_speech_end = 0\n\n    for i, is_speech in enumerate(speech_frames):\n        time = i * hop_length / sr\n        if is_speech and not in_speech:\n            # Check if we should switch speaker due to long silence\n            if time - last_speech_end > max_silence_duration:\n                current_speaker = (current_speaker + 1) % num_speakers\n            start_time = time\n            in_speech = True\n        elif not is_speech and in_speech:\n            duration = time - start_time\n            if duration > min_speech_duration:\n                diarization[Segment(start_time, time)] = f\"SPEAKER_{current_speaker:02d}\"\n                last_speech_end = time\n            in_speech = False\n\n    # Handle final speech segment\n    if in_speech:\n        duration = len(audio)/sr - start_time\n        if duration > min_speech_duration:\n            diarization[Segment(start_time, len(audio)/sr)] = f\"SPEAKER_{current_speaker:02d}\"\n\n    return diarization\n\ndef format_ass_time(seconds):\n    \"\"\"Format seconds to ASS time format H:MM:SS.cs\"\"\"\n    hours = int(seconds // 3600)\n    minutes = int((seconds % 3600) // 60)\n    secs = seconds % 60\n    cs = int((secs % 1) * 100)\n    return f\"{hours}:{minutes:02d}:{int(secs):02d}.{cs:02d}\"\n\ndef generate_rttm(diarization, audio_duration):\n    \"\"\"Generate RTTM format output.\"\"\"\n    rttm_lines = []\n    for turn, _, speaker in diarization.itertracks(yield_label=True):\n        start = turn.start\n        duration = turn.end - turn.start\n        # Format: SPEAKER file_name channel_num start_time duration <NA> <NA> speaker_id <NA> <NA>\n        speaker_id = speaker.replace(\"SPEAKER_\", \"spk\")\n        rttm_line = f\"SPEAKER input 1 {start:.3f} {duration:.3f} <NA> <NA> {speaker_id} <NA> <NA>\"\n        rttm_lines.append(rttm_line)\n    return rttm_lines\n\ndef merge_diarization_transcription(diarization, transcription):\n    \"\"\"Merge diarization results with transcription.\"\"\"\n    from pyannote.core import Segment\n\n    merged = []\n    segments = transcription.get(\"segments\", [])\n\n    for seg in segments:\n        start = seg.get(\"start\", 0)\n        end = seg.get(\"end\", 0)\n        text = seg.get(\"text\", \"\").strip()\n\n        if not text or start is None:\n            continue\n\n        # Find the dominant speaker in this segment\n        segment = Segment(start, end)\n        speaker_labels = {}\n        for turn, _, speaker in diarization.itertracks(yield_label=True):\n            overlap = segment.duration - segment.crop(turn).duration\n            if overlap > 0:\n                label = speaker\n                speaker_labels[label] = speaker_labels.get(label, 0) + overlap\n\n        # Get the speaker with maximum overlap\n        if speaker_labels:\n            dominant_speaker = max(speaker_labels, key=speaker_labels.get)\n            speaker_id = f\"SPEAKER_{int(dominant_speaker.split('_')[-1]) if '_' in dominant_speaker else 0:02d}\"\n        else:\n            speaker_id = \"SPEAKER_00\"\n\n        merged.append({\n            \"start\": start,\n            \"end\": end,\n            \"speaker\": speaker_id,\n            \"text\": text\n        })\n\n    return merged\n\ndef energy.\"\"\"\n    import torch\n    from pyannote.core import Annotation, Segment\n\n    # Load audio\n    audio, sr = librosa.load(audio_path, sr=16000)\n\n    # Compute energy for VAD\n    frame_length = 2048\n    hop_length = 512\n    energy = np.array([np.sum(audio[i:i+frame_length]**2) for i in range(0, len(audio)-frame_length, hop_length)])\n\n    # Adaptive threshold based on audio characteristics\n    if np.any(energy > 0):\n        threshold = np.mean(energy[energy > np.percentile(energy[energy > 0], 5)])\n    else:\n        threshold = 0\n\n    speech_frames = energy > threshold\n\n    # Create diarization annotation with speaker switching\n    diarization = Annotation()\n    current_speaker = 0\n    in_speech = False\n    start_time = 0\n    min_speech_duration = 0.5  # Minimum speech segment duration in seconds\n    max_silence_duration = 1.5  # Maximum silence before switching speakers\n\n    last_speech_end = 0\n\n    for i, is_speech in enumerate(speech_frames):\n        time = i * hop_length / sr\n        if is_speech and not in_speech:\n            # Check if we should switch speaker due to long silence\n            if time - last_speech_end > max_silence_duration:\n                current_speaker = (current_speaker + 1) % num_speakers\n            start_time = time\n            in_speech = True\n        elif not is_speech min_speech_duration:\n            diarization[Segment(start_time, len(audio)/sr)] = f\"SPEAKER_{current_speaker:02d}\"\n\n    return diarization\n\ndef format_ass_time(seconds):\n    \"\"\"Format seconds to ASS time format H:MM:SS.cs\"\"\"\n    hours = int(seconds // 3600)\n    minutes = int((seconds % 3600) // 60)\n    secs = seconds % 60\n    cs = int((secs % 1) * 100)\n    return \"text\": text\n        })\n\n    return merged\n\ndef generate_ass_subtitles(merged_segments, audio_duration):\n    \"\"\"Generate ASS subtitle format with speaker labels.\"\"\"\n    ass_lines = [\n        \"[Script Info]\",\n        \"ScriptType: v4.00+\",\n        \"Collisions: Normal\",\n        \"PlayDepth: 0\",\n        \"\",\n        \"[V4+ Styles]\",\n        \"Format: Name, Fontname, ass_lines.append(dialogue)\n\n    return ass_lines\n\ndef calculate_speech_time(diarization):\n    \"\"\"Calculate total speech time from diarization.\"\"\"\n    total_time = 0\n    for turn, _, _ in Configuration\nAUDIO_PATH = \"/root/audio.wav\"\nVIDEO_PATH = \"/root/input.mp4\"\nOUTPUT_DIR = \"/root\"\n\n# Simple annotation class for diarization\nclass Annotation:\n    \"\"\"Simple annotation class to replace pyannote.core.Annotation\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.segments = []\n\n    def __getitem__(self, key):\n        return self.segments\n\n    def itertracks(self, yield_label=False):\n        for seg in self.segments:\n            yield (seg['start'], seg['end']), 0, seg['label']\n\nclass Segment:\n    \"\"\"Simple segment class to replace pyannote.core.Segment\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, start, end):\n        self.start = start\n        self.end = end\n        self.duration = end - start\n\n    def crop(self, other):\n        \"\"\"Get overlap with another segment\"\"\"\n        start = max(self.start, other.start)\n        end = min(self.end, other.end)\n        if end > start:\n            return Segment(start, end)\n        return Segment(0, 0)\n\ndef get_audio_duration(audio_path):\n    \"\"\"Get audio duration using ffmpeg.\"\"\"\n    result = subprocess.run(\n        [\"ffprobe\", \"-v\", \"error\", run_simple_diarization(audio_path, num_speakers=2):\n    \"\"\"Run simple VAD-based speaker diarization using audio energy.\"\"\"\n    # Load audio\n    audio, sr = librosa.load(audio_path, sr=16000)\n\n    # Compute energy for VAD\n    frame_length = 2048\n    hop_length = 512\n    energy = np.array([np.sum(audio[i:i+frame_length]**2) for i in range(0, len(audio)-frame_length, hop_length)])\n\n    # Adaptive threshold based on audio characteristics\n    if np.any(energy > 0):\n        threshold = np.mean(energy[energy > np.percentile(energy[energy > 0], 5)])\n    else:\n        threshold = 0\n\n    speech_frames = energy > threshold\n\n    # Create diarization annotation with speaker switching\n    diarization = Annotation()\n    diarization.segments = []\n switching speakers\n\n    last_speech_end = 0\n\n    for i, is_speech in enumerate(speech_frames):\n        time = i * hop_length / sr\n        if is_speech and not in_speech:\n            # Check if we should switch speaker due to long silence\n            if time - last_speech_end > max_silence_duration:\n                current_speaker = (current_speaker + 1) % num_speakers\n            start_time = time\n            in_speech = True\n        elif not is_speech and in_speech:\n            duration = time - start_time\n            if duration > min_speech_duration:\n                diarization.segments.append({\n                    'start': Segment(start_time, time),\n                    'end': time,\n                    'label': f\"SPEAKER_{current_speaker:02d}\"\n generate_rttm(diarization, audio_duration):\n    \"\"\"Generate RTTM format output.\"\"\"\n    rttm_lines = []\n    for seg in diarization.segments:\n        start = seg['start'].start\n        end = audio, performs transcription and simple diarization, and generates output files.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nimport os\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport librosa\n\n# Configuration\nAUDIO_PATH = \"/root/audio.wav\"\nVIDEO_PATH = \"/root/input.mp4\"\nOUTPUT_DIR = \"/root\"\n\n# Simple annotation class for diarization\nclass Annotation:\n    \"\"\"Simple annotation class to replace pyannote.core.Annotation\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.segments = []\n\n    def __getitem__(self, key):\n        return self.segments\n\n    def itertracks(self, yield_label=False):\n        for seg in self.segments:\n            yield (seg['start'], seg['end']), 0, seg['label']\n\nclass Segment:\n    \"\"\"Simple segment class to replace pyannote.core.Segment\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, start, end):\n        self.start = start\n        self.end = end\n        self.duration = end - start\n\n    def crop(self, other):\n        \"\"\"Get overlap with another segment\"\"\"\n        start = max(self.start, other.start)\n        end = min(self.end, other.end)\n        if end > start:\n            return Segment(start, end)\n        return Segment(0, 0)\n\ndef get_audio_duration(audio_path):\n    \"\"\"Get audio duration using ffmpeg.\"\"\"\n    result = subprocess.run(\n        [\"ffprobe\", \"-v\", \"error\", \"-show_entries\", \"format=duration\", \"-of\", \"default=noprint_wrappers=1:nokey=1\", audio_path],\n        capture_output=True, text=True\n    )\n    return float(result.stdout.strip())\n\ndef subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, check=True)\n    return get_audio_duration(audio_path)\n\ndef run_transcription(audio_path):\n    \"\"\"Run transcription using OpenAI Whisper.\"\"\"\n    import torch\n    import whisper\n\n    device = \"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\"\n    print(f\"Using device: {device}\")\n\n    # Load Whisper model\n    model = device\n\ndef run_simple_diarization(audio_path, num_speakers=2):\n    \"\"\"Run simple VAD-based speaker diarization using audio energy.\"\"\"\n    # Load audio\n    audio, sr = librosa.load(audio_path, sr=16000)\n\n    # Compute energy for VAD\n    frame_length = 2048\n    hop_length = 512\n    energy = np.array([np.sum(audio[i:i+frame_length]**2) for i in range(0, len(audio)-frame_length, hop_length)])\n\n    # Adaptive threshold based on audio characteristics\n    if np.any(energy > 0):\n        threshold = np.mean(energy[energy > np.percentile(energy[energy > 0], 5)])\n    else:\n        threshold = 0\n\n    speech_frames = energy > threshold\n\n    # Create diarization annotation with speaker switching\n    diarization = Annotation()\n    diarization.segments = []\n    current_speaker = 0\n    in_speech = False\n    start_time = 0\n    min_speech_duration = 0.5  # Minimum speech segment duration in seconds\n    max_silence_duration = 1.5  # Maximum silence before switching speakers\n\n    last_speech_end = 0\n\n    for i, is_speech in enumerate(speech_frames):\n        time = i * hop_length / sr\n        if is_speech and not in_speech:\n            # Check if we should switch speaker due to long silence\n            if time - last_speech_end > max_silence_duration:\n                current_speaker = (current_speaker + 1) % num_speakers\n            start_time = time\n            in_speech = True\n        elif not is_speech and in_speech:\n            duration = time - start_time\n            if duration > min_speech_duration:\n                diarization.segments.append({\n                    'start': Segment(start_time, time),\n                    'end': time,\n                    'label': f\"SPEAKER_{current_speaker:02d}\"\n                })\n                last_speech_end = time\n            in_speech = False\n\n    # Handle final speech segment\n    if in_speech:\n        duration
